### 2026-09-17 — Task 12: labels → ARB keys (localization sweep) — COMPLETE

- **Answer to the question that started it:** the Settings *screens* were NOT keyed. Only their titles/section names existed in `app_en.arb` / `app_bn.arb` (`settingsScreen_*`, `settings*_title`); every body label on Settings, Account & Profile, Profile, Manage Account, Online Sync, Backup & Restore and the whole Edit Court/Steps tree was a hard-coded English string in Dart, so editing the ARB could not change it. Both files did already agree with each other (256 keys, same order, no key present in only one language).
- **Now:** those labels are keys and are read through `AppLocalizations`. **137 keys added (256 → 393)**; the earlier count of hard-coded user-facing literals was ~150 across `lib/`, and all but the dev-only scratch screens (`core/scratch`, `features/scratch`) and the four `— Task 12`/`— Task 20` placeholder bodies are now keyed. Non-labels deliberately left alone: the `—` empty-value dash, the `+880` phone prefix, the `1XXXXXXXXX` phone-format hint, mockup placeholder data (`Dhaka Bar Association`, `1557`, `Chamber 402 …`, `1812332721`, Manage Account's `Asadusjaman` / `advocate.asad@law.com`) and route/DB ids.
- **Organised by screen:** both ARB files were rewritten so keys are contiguous per screen, each screen preceded by an `@@_section_<id>` marker row whose value names the section and the source file, e.g. `"@@_section_settingsBackupRestore": "SETTINGS - BACKUP & RESTORE  |  lib/features/settings/presentation/backup_restore_screen.dart"`. Flutter's ARB parser ignores `@`-prefixed keys, so the markers are invisible to `flutter gen-l10n` (verified with a real gen-l10n run) and to the repo's own `tool/generate_l10n_dart*.py` / `generate_l10n.py`, which skip them too. Consequence: the three checked-in generated files (`app_localizations*.dart`) are re-ordered because gen-l10n emits getters in ARB order — large but purely mechanical diff.
- **Section order** follows the app: APP WIDE → NAVIGATION → per case screen → ADD/EDIT CASE SHEET → CASES MODAL → CONTACT SHEET → NOTES → CASE REFERENCE → SYNC → SHARED BUTTONS/DIALOGS → APP SHELL → DATABASE RECOVERY → SEED DATA (court categories / on-behalf / case types / steps / divisions) → MESSAGE TEMPLATES → then the SETTINGS cluster (main list → Account & Profile → Profile → Manage Account → Online Sync → Backup & Restore → Edit Court/Steps → Edit Court Names & Categories → Court Names, Steps etc → Add/Edit Item → Court list → Divisions/Districts/Lower Court Categories → Notifications/View/Guidelines/About) → dev-only localization scratch.
- **Wired in code** (all labels now read from the ARB, English values unchanged so no test needed rewriting): `settings_main_screen.dart` — section + item titles are built per frame from the localizations (search now matches in the active language; new `settingsScreen_section*`, `*_searchTooltip`, `*_searchHint`, `*_closeSearchTooltip`, `*_noResults`); `accounts_profile_menu_screen.dart`, `profile_screen.dart` (data rows, edit sheet, `settingsProfile_displayName('Adv. {name}')`, save toast), `manage_account_screen.dart`, `online_sync_screen.dart` (incl. `{count} Items`), `backup_restore_screen.dart` (schedule options + section headings, now upper-cased in the widget so translations stay sentence-case), `edit_court_steps_menu.dart`, `edit_court_names_category_screen.dart`, `court_names_steps_screen.dart` (the 8 category labels), `item_list_screen.dart` (kind titles, short category labels, dialogs, empty/error states), `court_list_screen.dart`, `court_category_screen.dart`, `district_list_screen.dart` (`{division} Division`), `lower_court_category_list_screen.dart` (`{district} Court Categories`), `language_screen.dart` (reuses `settingsScreen_languageEnglish/Bengali`; **note** the English UI now shows "Bengali" rather than the autonym "বাংলা" — change the `en` value if the autonym is wanted), `app_router.dart` (exit dialog: `appShell_*`), `database_recovery_dialog.dart`, `app.dart` (`onGenerateTitle` → `appTitle`), and the six case screens (`casesContact_*`, `casesScreen_loadError`, `caseDetailsScreen_deleteCaseError`, `caseDetailsScreen_deleteHistoryError`, `caseDetailsScreen_loadError`).
- **Guard test** `test/l10n/arb_keys_test.dart` (4 tests): en/bn declare the same keys in the same order, no empty values, bn keeps every placeholder the en message uses (ICU plural/select messages skipped — a naive parser reads `=1{...}` as a placeholder), and the settings section markers exist. Also `test/features/profile_picture_test.dart` and `test/database/database_guard_test.dart` now supply `AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates` (those screens read the ARB now; the harnesses had bare `MaterialApp`s).
- `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` **207 passed, 2 skipped** (was 203 + 2). `dart format` applied to the touched files only; 12 files the formatter also wanted to reformat (pre-existing style drift: `settings_viewmodel.dart`, `disposed_help_dialog.dart`, `history_entry_edit_dialog.dart`, the `settings_*` barrels, …) were reverted so the diff stays scoped. Version `0.0.174`. **Resume point:** Task 12 Phase 3 (remaining Edit Court/Steps polish); the four `— Task 12` placeholder screens (Notifications, View Settings, Guidelines, About) still hold dev placeholder text and were not keyed.

### 2026-09-16 — Task 12 Phase 3: Lower Court Categories sub-menu — COMPLETE

- **New card** in Settings → Edit Court/Steps (`edit_court_steps_menu.dart`): **Lower Court Categories** (`account_tree_outlined`, "Divisions, districts and their court categories") → `AppRoutes.settingsLowerCourtCategories`, which is the existing Divisions picker with `?flow=lower_court_categories`. The shared Divisions/Districts pickers (`court_category_screen.dart`, `district_list_screen.dart`) gained an optional `flow` and route into this walkthrough; without it they keep the Edit Court Names & Categories behaviour. New route constant `AppRoutes.settingsLowerCourtCategoryList`.
- **New screen** `edit_court_steps/lower_court_category_list_screen.dart` — for one division + district it lists exactly the categories the Add/Edit Case form offers: pre-seeded lower categories with ≥1 court in that district (no court in `pre_seeded_data.dart` → hidden) plus the categories the user created for that district, all in the form's `order_index` serial. Rows reuse the court-name row layout (drag handle, body-lg title, edit + delete). Drag & drop persists `order_index` through the slots the district already occupies (so other districts and the form keep their relative order); edit renames (`name_key`, skipped when unchanged so a built-in keeps its localization key); delete is rendered **only on user-added categories** because `LocalCourtCategoriesRepository.delete` refuses built-ins. The FAB matches the Add Case button exactly — `backgroundColor: Colors.black`, `foregroundColor: Colors.white`, `Icon(Icons.add, size: 28)`, default bottom-right location — and inserts a custom category scoped to that district.
- **New shared helper** `lib/features/settings/data/district_court_categories.dart`: `districtCourtCategories` (the derivation both this screen and the form use), `nextLowerCourtCategoryOrderIndex`, and `reorderedCategoryOrderIndexes`.
- **Schema v7**: `court_categories` gained nullable `division` + `district` (`court_categories_table.dart`, migration `from < 7` in `app_database.dart`; Drift code regenerated). `court_categories_repository.dart` now reports `division`/`district` as changed fields. Custom categories are therefore **district-scoped**, exactly as the user asked; built-ins stay global (null scope).
- **Add/Edit Case form** (`shared/widgets/add_edit_case_sheet.dart`): `_lowerCategories` now calls the shared helper, so a category added in Settings shows up in the form's Court Category dropdown for that district only. Its inline filter/court-count logic was replaced by the helper.
- **Tests** `test/features/lower_court_category_test.dart` (15): derivation (hidden 0-court categories, district-scoped customs, higher-court exclusion), the **real pre-seeded hierarchy** (Dhaka → civil, sessions, metropolitan sessions, CJM, CMM, executive in that order; Gopalganj → civil, sessions, CJM, executive, i.e. the metropolitan categories are omitted — the seed has no tribunals category at all), ordering helpers, repository district scope + built-in delete refusal, screen listing/add/rename/delete/FAB parity, and the form offering the district's custom category. `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` 203 passed, 2 skipped. Version `0.0.173`.
- Notes for the next session: the reorder **interaction** is covered by unit tests on the slot mapping, not by an end-to-end drag test; renaming a built-in writes a literal `name_key`, so that category then shows the same text in Bengali as in English (the add/rename dialog also short-circuits when the text is unchanged, which protects built-ins from an accidental wipe). Drift-backed widget tests leave a pending stream timer at teardown, so this screen's widget tests inject in-memory fakes instead — a `tearDown` that closes a Drift database in a `testWidgets` test can deadlock. **Resume point:** Task 12 Phase 3 (remaining Edit Court/Steps polish).

### 2026-09-05 — Case Detail → Case History display text fix — COMPLETE

- In `_historyEntry`, the content `Column` was `crossAxisAlignment: start`, which let the Step/Result `Text` cap at the longest-word width and wrap after ~two words instead of using the full available width. ### 2026-09-02 — Task 09 fixes (post-Phase 4, 2): calendar selection off-by-one + dialog height — COMPLETE

- Bug: selecting a date in the calendar picked the previous day (e.g. tapping 03/09/2026 selected 02/09/2026). Root cause: `AppDateUtils.bangladeshCivilDateEpoch` built the civil-day start-of-day from a **local**-midnight `DateTime(year, month, day)`, which on a UTC+6 (Bangladesh) device shifts the epoch back ~6h into the prior UTC day. Fixed to use `DateTime.utc(year, month, day)` so the cell epoch matches the labeled civil date on any device timezone. This also keeps grid cell keys consistent with `normalizeToBangladeshStartOfDay` (used by the badge counts), so badges stay correct.
- Enhancement: reduced the calendar dialog height by ~5% only (`minHeight` 0.70→0.665, `maxHeight` 0.92→0.874 of screen height). Width unchanged (360–460).

Version `0.0.115`. Known issues: none.

### 2026-09-02 — Task 09 fixes (post-Phase 4): badge calendar badges, black screen, dialog size — COMPLETE

- Bug: date tap opened a black screen. Root cause: `_DayCell.onTap` both invoked `widget.onDateSelected(epoch)` (which already pops the dialog with the value) AND called `Navigator.of(context).pop()` again, double-popping the route and sending the app back to a black state. Fixed by removing the redundant `Navigator.pop()` — the dialog closes once via the single pop in the screen's `onDateSelected` callback.
- Bug: badges never showed even on dates with cases. Root cause: `CasesDao.countByNextDateRange` grouped by the raw stored `nextDate` epoch, which only equals `bangladeshCivilDateEpoch(day)` when the device is in Bangladesh timezone. Fixed by normalizing each count key with `AppDateUtils.normalizeToBangladeshStartOfDay`, and normalizing the screen's month range bounds, so grid lookups match on any device timezone.
- Enhancement: enlarged the calendar dialog (`minHeight` 0.7 → `maxHeight` 0.92 of screen height, width 360–460) and made day cells taller (`childAspectRatio` 1 → 1.25) so the month grid is larger. The selected non-today date now uses a visible black ring (was an invisible secondary ring).

Version `0.0.114`. No remaining known issues in the Daily Cause List / badge calendar.

Version `0.0.107`.

### 2026-08-25 — Fixed Disposed card date rendering — COMPLETE

- The date under "Disposed:" was showing the literal "Disposed:" text instead of the date for cases disposed today/tomorrow. Cause: `formatDisplayDate` was called with `todayLabel`/`tomorrowLabel` set to `l.caseCard_disposed` ("Disposed:"), so when the next date matched today/tomorrow it returned that label verbatim. Switched to `formatDateDDMMYYYY` so the line always renders the actual date (DD/MM/YYYY).

Version `0.0.106`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposed card: moved Next Date under "Disposed:" label — COMPLETE

Version `0.0.105`.

- Updated `test/shared/widgets/phase3_widgets_test.dart`: dropped the disposed-card assertion and fixed the red-border test (it now sets `isOverdue: true`, which was a pre-existing failure unrelated to this change).

Version `0.0.104`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposed Cases: rebuilt card to 7-line layout — COMPLETE

- Replaced the shared `CaseCard` (disposed variant, which showed a history timeline) with a dedicated `_DisposedCaseCard` matching the required 7-line spec: Step/Result chip + "Disposed:" label (L1), Case Type & Number + last Next Date (L2), Petitioner Vs Respondent (L3), Case Title/Section (L4), Court + judges (L5), Note in a quoted box with secondary left border (L6, per the mockup), and call/message/update actions (L7).
- Removed the now-dead history fetching (`_historyById`, `CaseHistoryDao`) from this screen. The shared `CaseCard.disposed` variant is left in place (unused here) to avoid unrelated churn.

Version `0.0.103`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposed Cases: auto-reload after Quick Update — COMPLETE

- Root cause: `_openQuickUpdate` opened `QuickUpdateCaseDialog` but did not reload afterward. Reviving a case (removing disposal steps via Quick Update) left it in the Disposed list until a manual pull-to-refresh.
- Fix: added `if (mounted) await _loadCases();` after the dialog closes — matching the existing pattern in `all_cases_screen.dart`. The list now reflects the change immediately.

Version `0.0.102`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposal detection used wrong label set — COMPLETE

- Root cause: `CasesDao` matched the Steps field against a hardcoded two-word list `['disposed', 'নিষ্পত্তি']` (`_disposedLabels`). But `last_step` stores the actual comma-joined **step names** (e.g. `"Withdrawal,Dismissal"`), so selecting disposal steps like Withdrawal/Dismissal/Allowed/Rejected never matched and the case stayed in Active/Upcoming/Overdue.
- Fix: derived `disposedStepNames` (both languages) from `pre_seeded_data.disposedStepIds` via `stepLabelEn`/`stepLabelBn`, and switched `_stepIsDisposed`/`_stepIsNotDisposed` to match against that full set with substring `LIKE`. Now any case whose Steps contain a disposal step name is correctly treated as disposed.

Version `0.0.101`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposal detection: match label inside mixed Step/Result — COMPLETE

- Root cause: `CasesDao._isDisposed` matched `lastStep` with `isIn(_disposedLabels)` (exact equality). District-court steps are stored comma-joined (e.g. `"Disposed, Argument"`), so a disposed step mixed with others failed the exact match and the case was treated as active.
- Fix: disposal now uses `LIKE '%disposed%'` / `'%নিষ্পত্তি%'` substring matching for both the Steps field and the AD/HCD Result field. A case is disposed whenever either field *contains* a disposal label, even alongside other steps/words. The active-case count queries were updated to use the new `_stepIsNotDisposed` expression.

Version `0.0.100`.

### 2026-08-25 — Keep disposed cases out of non-disposed screens — COMPLETE

- Root cause: `CasesDao.getByNextDateRange` did not exclude disposed cases, so disposed cases leaked into Upcoming, Overdue, and Daily Cause List screens.
- Fix: added `_isDisposed.not()` to the date-range query. Disposed cases now appear only in Active (excluded) and Disposed (only-source) screens, plus the unfiltered All Cases screen — matching the intended scope.

Version `0.0.99`.

### 2026-08-25 — Disposed Cases card: enable Update button — COMPLETE

- The Disposed Cases card's Update button was disabled (no `onUpdate` handler). Wired `_openQuickUpdate` → `QuickUpdateCaseDialog.show`, so a disposed case can now be revived/edited if needed. Added the `add_edit_case_sheet.dart` import.

Version `0.0.98`.

### 2026-08-25 — Overdue date styling across case cards — COMPLETE

- Added a per-case `CaseCard.isOverdue` flag. All/Active hosts compute it from `nextDate < bangladeshTodayStartOfDay()`; Disposed passes `true` (past by definition).
- When `isOverdue`: All/Active cards show the red left stripe and the date label switches to "Previous Date" with both label and value rendered in red (`AppColors.error`). Disposed cards relabel their date "Previous Date" (also red).
- Overdue screen's Case Type/Case Number is now black (`AppColors.onSurface`) instead of red; stripe, "Previous Date" label, and red date text remain.
- `flutter analyze` clean repo-wide.

Version `0.0.97`.
